# WoW Forever open-world elite and group quest chains — through minimum acceptance level 59

> **Get the chain. Then get the group.** Collect the solo setup before tackling open-world elite objectives.

Updated: 2026-11-14

341 unique quests across 36 elite chain sections (341 section entries). Repeated quest IDs are intentional; completing one ID completes that quest everywhere.

## Scope and evidence

Curated, source-backed preparation guide: 36 coherent outdoor chain/questing clusters and 341 individually identified quest records across Alliance, Horde and shared routes. Includes classic-origin quests and new Forever content in Wetlands, Riverglades, Zephras Isle, Shen'dralas and Mount Hyjal. Covers early group bounties through high-fifties payoffs; quest-level-60 entries remain only when documented acceptance is below 60. Two named Hyjal preparation/follow-up records retain unknown acceptance rather than guessed minima. Dungeon and raid objectives, known minimum-60-only quests, and numerical XP promises are excluded. Curated coverage, not an exhaustive list or an optimal XP ranking.

WoW Forever can differ from other versions of WoW. Minimum acceptance level is not quest level. Confirm uncertain requirements in game using the linked evidence. This guide is not an eligibility guarantee.

- Compiled from current public ForeverChanges and Icy Veins quest records, Questie Forever generated data, maintained corrections and observed quest/NPC traces. Not personally verified by running every chain on the live server. No XP amount, reward upgrade or live availability is promised.
- Every level_range is an editorial recommendation for the group payoff/questing route, NOT the minimum unlock level. min_level is the source-listed minimum for that individual quest; earlier prerequisites can impose a higher effective entry level. Some quest-level-60 outdoor finales are obtainable before 60 and need a prepared high-fifties group.
- Required means a prerequisite is listed in the consulted source, not that the server was personally tested. AND/ALL groups require every named quest; ONE/OR groups are alternatives. Observed handoffs alone do not prove hard gates. None documented means no gate found in consulted records, not proof of no gate. Unknown preserves conflicting or missing evidence.
- Questie relations infer order from player sessions. The current relation snapshot is dated 2026-11-12; quest/NPC traces are dated 2026-11-13. Some Hyjal gates have fewer than ten contributing characters. Generated and maintained corrections also contain explicit assumptions/unverified faction masks; these are not authoritative server metadata.
- Classic-origin objectives are retained only with a current Forever-facing source and are marked provisional where that source expressly reuses Classic text. Forever can change camps, NPC locations, group difficulty and quest gates; Classic equivalence is never assumed as live verification.
- Before-travel objects are faction-specific; Both means general/shared advice. Race-specific introductions are called out in notes because faction filters cannot represent every race restriction. Zephras shared access and several Hyjal Both masks remain provisional.
- Wetlands correction: Nek'rosh's Gambit needs War Banners AND From the Ashes; This Land Was Their Land is observed after Defeat Nek'rosh AND Old Habits; Death to the Dragonmaw 98291 needs Razormaw Needling AND Trying Times; Stopping the Cycle 98297 follows it. The Halloran supply branch is preserved separately instead of collapsing the whole route into a short Howin list.
- Wetlands pickup correction: Forced Disarmament starts from an armament object in Dragonmaw Retreat and returns to Stoutfist. Bring Back a Bang is from Ashlan Stonesmirk, while Spark of Freedom starts with the Captive Fire Elemental and returns to Roggo. The updated Wetlands guide is useful route context but some NPC attributions/shortened chains conflict with direct records; direct records and explicit traces take precedence.
- Faction conflicts: ForeverChanges currently labels Carnage 98283, Hearts of the Lost 87318 and Crocs of the Sky 98072 Both; Questie/adjacent chain evidence supports conservative Alliance placement here. Their notes retain the disagreement. Crocs of the Sky also has a 10-versus-8 scale-count conflict; follow the live counter.
- Outdoor finales with an external dungeon prerequisite are clearly labeled: Saving Sharpbeak, The Ancient Egg and Summoning Shadra need Zul'Farrak preparation upstream. No dungeon objective quest record is included. Likewise The Dark Iron War's Stockade follow-up is mentioned as a planning handoff, not included as outdoor work.
- Not every record is tagged Elite: solo collection, delivery, consultation and escort setup is deliberately retained so a party does not arrive at a locked finale. Group-friendly pirate/salvage areas are labeled as such rather than inventing an Elite classification. A source-tagged Elite delivery is not itself necessarily a fight.
- Coordinates are approximate, zone/map-specific and sometimes conflicting. Prefer the named NPC and quest text. Icy Veins and Questie differ on Fayran/Felflick coordinates; Curator Thorius is in Ironforge despite a Dun Morogh map label. Halt the Hail is physically in Winterspring although some indexes file it under Mount Hyjal.
- Unknown acceptance: A Fitting End 96435 and A Revenge Now Bitter 96322 are named, objective-backed Hyjal preparation/follow-up records, not placeholders. Their current public pages/traces do not establish a minimum acceptance level, so min_level is null; they are not evidence of a guaranteed pre-60 offer.
- Not exhaustive: additional new-zone lines, rare-drop starters, alternate quest versions, race/class routes and live changes may exist. Similar-name alternate IDs are not assumed to award independent repeat rewards. Consult the linked records and current NPC offers before a long journey.
- The consulted Daily Respawn Wetlands route identifies itself as AI-authored and source-checked. It is used only for route context, never as sole authority over conflicting direct quest records or maintained Questie corrections.

Dataset sources: [1. foreverchanges.pro](<https://foreverchanges.pro/quests>) · [2. db.icy-veins.com](<https://db.icy-veins.com/wow-forever/quests>) · [3. raw.githubusercontent.com](<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Questie/QuestieDB/master/data/Forever/foreverQuestDB.lua>) · [4. raw.githubusercontent.com](<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Questie/QuestieDB/master/src/corrections/Forever/generated/foreverBaseQuest.lua>) · [5. raw.githubusercontent.com](<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Questie/QuestieDB/master/src/corrections/Forever/foreverQuestFixes.lua>) · [6. raw.githubusercontent.com](<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Questie/QuestieDB/master/src/corrections/Forever/traces/foreverQuestRelationTraces.lua>) · [7. raw.githubusercontent.com](<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Questie/QuestieDB/master/src/corrections/Forever/traces/foreverQuestTraces.lua>) · [8. raw.githubusercontent.com](<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Questie/QuestieDB/master/src/corrections/Forever/traces/foreverNpcTraces.lua>) · [9. dailyrespawn.com](<https://dailyrespawn.com/article/wetlands-leveling-guide-wow-forever-menethil-harbor-quest-order-levels-20-30>) · [10. forever-xp.com](<https://forever-xp.com/quests/98297/>)

## Legend and use

- **Required** — complete the documented prerequisite chain before the target quest becomes available.
- **Optional breadcrumb** — a lead-in or routing quest, not a documented hard gate.
- **None documented** — no prerequisite was found in the consulted sources; this does not prove that none exists.
- **Unknown** — the requirement is unresolved or conflicting; confirm in game.
- **Min. ?** — minimum acceptance level is unconfirmed. The interactive guide keeps these quests visible at every level cap.
- **Heading level range** — recommended group levels, not minimum acceptance or entry requirements.
- **Faction / class** — class-only restrictions apply to both factions unless a faction is explicitly included. Unrestricted quests apply to every class of the named faction.
- Complete each elite chain’s **Before you travel** checklist, then collect the target quests. Checkmarks in this Markdown copy are independent of the browser checklist.
- The [interactive checklist](elites.html) defaults to Alliance, Warlock, and maximum acceptance level 59. It supports search, filters, and browser-local progress keyed by unique quest ID, shared across both guides. This Markdown copy contains every dataset entry and all faction variants, without those filters.

## Zephras Isle: Vulgara — Levels 8–11

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Forever-only starter-zone elite bounty. Questie supplies an observed notice starter; its all-races mask is an explicit database assumption, not live verification.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — Finish the Alliance Welcome to Shen'dar Village 93461 before the Vulgara bounty; current Questie corrections list it as an alternative prerequisite to the Horde introduction. Read the bounty notice near Shen'dar Village, kill Vulgara in Shen'dar Highlands, then return her head to Danarii Bellowveil.
- [ ] **Horde** — Finish the available Horde Welcome to Shen'dar Village 92514 before the Vulgara bounty. Its generated race mask is Tauren, so availability for every Horde race is not established. The bounty itself is provisionally shared; do not assume travel to the island alone bypasses the introduction.

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

## Darkshore: Gyromast and the prospector — Levels 18–22

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Two independent outdoor group errands at opposite ends of Darkshore.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — Visit Gelkak Gyromast on the northern coast: Gyromast's Retrieval → Gyromast's Revenge. Finish the collection before assembling for the machine encounter.
- [ ] **Alliance** — In the south, The Absent Minded Prospector referral leads to Prospector Remtravel. Clear the excavation camp before starting his escort; this is a separate route, not a gate for Gyromast.

## Silverpine: Pyrewood and Fenris Isle — Levels 18–25

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Horde outdoor ambush and Fenris group objectives, including the new Offering of Blood. These are independent routes; consult the current NPC offer for new-quest gates.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Horde** — Pyrewood Ambush starts with Deathstalker Faerleia at Pyrewood: prepare for waves before accepting. For Assault on Fenris Isle, prepare The Dead Fields → The Decrepit Ferry → Rot Hide Ichor → Rot Hide Origins → Thule Ravenclaw → Report to Hadrec. The Engraved Ring / Raleigh branch is separate, not the documented gate for the assault.
- [ ] **Horde** — The new Offering of Blood is a separate ritual finale at the Offering Stone in Beren's Peril (about 58.0,69.8). Finish The Tortured Soul 95883 by collecting the soul from nearby undead first; current Questie corrections require it before The Offering of Blood 95885. This is not proven to unlock merely by completing the old Fenris assault.

<a id="loch-modan-ogres"></a>

## Loch Modan: Mo’grosh ogres and Ol’ Sooty — Levels 19–23

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Early dwarf-region group objectives that can be missed when leaving Loch Modan for Wetlands.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — At Thelsamar, take Mercenaries from Magistrate Bluntnose and the WANTED: Chok'sul notice before entering the Mo'grosh ogre compound. These are parallel pickups, not a proven linear gate.
- [ ] **Alliance** — At the Farstrider Lodge, prepare Vyrin's Revenge for Ol' Sooty. Complete the follow-up dialogue after the bear kill; the two same-name quest records are not duplicate bounties.

<a id="barrens-group-bounties"></a>

## The Barrens: Counterattack and new bounties — Levels 19–24

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Classic defense event plus new Forever named-elite bounties; no instance objectives.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Horde** — At the northern Barrens bunker, finish Regthar Deathgate's centaur errands before Counterattack! Gather the party before triggering the defense; also check the wanted notice for Bruuz.
- [ ] **Horde** — Chol'aruk the Ravener has a Horde quest record; collect it before hunting, not after the kill.
- [ ] **Alliance** — Chol'aruk the Ravener has a separate Alliance quest ID. Follow your faction's pickup rather than assuming the Horde turn-in works.

<a id="redridge-stonewatch"></a>

## Redridge: Stonewatch and Morganth — Levels 24–28

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Dense elite camp with separate bounty, orc-leader and tower chains; Forever adds a prerequisite and an extra named bounty.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — In Lakeshire, take Blackrock Menace AND the new Blackrock Blockade before Tharil'zun; current Questie traces report both as gates. Stack the Gath'Ilzogg and Incinerator Gar'im wanted notices before clearing Stonewatch.
- [ ] **Alliance** — For Morganth, start A Watchful Eye from Theocritus at the Tower of Azora in Elwynn, then Looking Further at the Old Lion Statue in Redridge. Read the relevant object before the tower assault; do Shadow Magic on the same trip.
- [ ] **Alliance** — Missing In Action is Corporal Keeshan's outdoor escort. Clear his camp and agree when to start the event; it is not a prerequisite for every Stonewatch bounty.

## Hillsbrad: the Dun Garok push — Levels 29–33

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Horde chain-heavy dwarf fortress route: prepare outside the elite camp, then combine its kill, sword and elixir objectives.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Horde** — Start Battle of Hillsbrad with High Executor Darthalia in Tarren Mill and complete the successive farm/town objectives before the Dun Garok step. Take Humbert's Sword from Deathguard Humbert before entering the dwarf fortress.
- [ ] **Horde** — Apothecary Lydon's Elixir of Agony is a separate multi-step preparation line; finish the earlier ingredient/delivery steps before its Dun Garok payoff. Do not assume the first quest of the same name is the elite step.

<a id="wetlands-dragonmaw"></a>

## Wetlands: Dragonmaw Retreat and Stopping the Cycle — Levels 29–35

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

The requested new Forever dragonkin route, with the older Nek'rosh route and Halloran supply branch retained so the elite payoff is not missed.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — Before leaving Menethil, prepare BOTH branches: James Halloran's Young Crocolisk Skins → Apprentice's Duties → Crocs of the Sky → Crimson Crate Delivery to Howin Kindfeather; and Captain Stoutfist's War Banners AND From the Ashes → Nek'rosh's Gambit → Defeat Nek'rosh. Questie reports War Banners and From the Ashes as parallel AND prerequisites for the catapult step, not proof that one directly unlocks the other. Do the collection and attack-plan errands before the elite push.
- [ ] **Alliance** — After Nek'rosh, inspect the nearby book for Old Habits. Questie observed traces require BOTH Old Habits and Defeat Nek'rosh before This Land Was Their Land; do not treat a guide's short Howin list as the complete unlock route.
- [ ] **Alliance** — At Howin Kindfeather off the eastern road (about 49.4,42.1), finish BOTH Razormaw Needling and Trying Times before Death to the Dragonmaw 98291. Questie reports an AND gate. Collect Forced Disarmament from its armament object inside Dragonmaw Retreat, not from Howin; return those armaments to Captain Stoutfist.
- [ ] **Alliance** — Return to Howin after the Dragonmaw kills for Stopping the Cycle 98297: kill the Subdued Dragonspawn at the Dragonmaw Gates. The updated guide advises killing the controlling warlocks first. Word to Ironforge is the follow-up delivery. Stack Rethiel's Carnage nearby, but it is a separate raptor encounter, not a proven Dragonmaw prerequisite.

<a id="wetlands-dun-modr"></a>

## Wetlands: Dark Iron dwarves, Dun Modr and Thandol Span — Levels 30–35

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

The requested dwarf route: classic Dark Iron/Dun Modr and bridge chain plus new Forever explosives, elemental escort, lost-dwarf and guardian-golem payoffs.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — Pick up Fall of Dun Modr from Harlo Barnaby in Menethil, then travel to the dwarf camp at Dun Modr. Motley Garmason gives The Dark Iron War (minimum 25); Longbraid the Grim gives A Grim Task for Balgaras. Stack Ashlan Stonesmirk's new Bring Back a Bang (about 50.1,18.2), then look for the Captive Fire Elemental's Spark of Freedom escort in the Dark Iron area; the escort returns to Roggo Harlbarrow. The individual quest records correct an updated guide that assigns the explosives pickup to Roggo.
- [ ] **Alliance** — At Dun Modr, Rhag Garmason starts The Thandol Span 631 → 632 → 633, then Plea To The Alliance 634 sends you onward. Inspect the missing dwarf at the broken bridge when asked; do not substitute a generic Dark Iron kill for the investigation.
- [ ] **Alliance** — For the new dwarf rescue line, Corma Villard starts Hearts of the Lost → Essene Villard gives Accessories of the Lost → Sentiments of the Lost returns her note. Search Thandol Span and its river approaches, not the dungeon. Questie marks the starter Alliance even though ForeverChanges currently says Both.
- [ ] **Alliance** — Before the golem excursion, finish Uncovering the Past with Prospector Whelgar at the excavation → Understanding Our Present → Gleaning Our Future → For Further Study (Ironforge delivery). Carry/use the Goaz Stone as instructed. These are outdoor guardian objectives; the Excavation Site dungeon quests are deliberately not included.
- [ ] **Alliance** — The Dark Iron War also unlocks The Fury Runs Deep for the Stockade. That dungeon objective is outside this catalog; take the follow-up if you are also using the dungeon guide.

<a id="duskwood-elite-payoffs"></a>

## Duskwood: Morbent Fel, Mor’Ladim and the Bride — Levels 30–36

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Three separate high-payoff outdoor finales. The listed records identify the important handoffs; prerequisite fields retain the upstream errands.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — Start Sven's Raven Hill investigation at his camp north-west of the Raven Hill cemetery in Duskwood, and follow the Darkshire / Stormwind handoffs. The final preparation is Lightforge Iron → The Lost Ingots → Blessed Arm → Armed and Ready → Morbent Fel. Carry Morbent's Bane for the final encounter; the solo setup is not optional.
- [ ] **Alliance** — Read A Weathered Grave at Raven Hill, return to Sirra Von'Indi in Darkshire for Morgan Ladimore, then take Mor'Ladim from Commander Althea Ebonlocke. Separately start the Hermit/Embalmer errands with Abercrombie beside Raven Hill; finish that long Darkshire delivery line through Translation to Ello → Bride of the Embalmer before looking for the Bride.

## Alterac: Syndicate leaders and Crushridge — Levels 36–42

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Outdoor Alterac group routes, separate from the new Dalaran dungeon quest chains.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — At Southshore, prepare Magistrate Henry Maleb's Syndicate errands and Marshal Redpath's Crushridge Bounty → Crushridge Warmongers. The Syndicate route uses Syndicate Assassins / Noble Deaths and Assassin's Contract before Baron's Demise and Dark Council; inspect the dropped contract when directed.
- [ ] **Horde** — Start The Crown of Will with Melisara in Tarren Mill, then follow each successive same-name step for Mug'thol's ogres. Keep the later ogre-leader fight for the group; the introductory delivery is not the elite payoff.

<a id="arathi-stromgarde"></a>

## Arathi: Stromgarde, Trelane and Trol’kalar — Levels 37–43

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

A major mid-level elite hub with different faction preparations and multiple same-trip objectives.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — At Refuge Pointe, take Stromgarde Badges and the Marez Cowl / Otto and Falconcrest wanted bounties before entering Stromgarde. Archmage Malin's introductory Malin's Request 690 starts in the Stormwind Mage Quarter; then prepare Worth Its Weight in Gold → Wand over Fist → Trelane's Defenses → An Apprentice's Enchantment → Attack on the Tower → the later Malin's Request 697. Finish the collection/travel setup before the tower assault; the two Malin records are not duplicates.
- [ ] **Horde** — At Hammerfall, prepare the sigil line: Sigil of Strom → The Broken Sigil / Sigil of Thoradin → Sigil of Arathor → Sigil of Trollbane → Trol'kalar. Follow the current NPC handoffs; do not assume all sigils are offered concurrently. Stack Foul Magics and the later Call to Arms / The Real Threat objectives if available.
- [ ] **Both** — Ground to Keep is a new Forever group objective in the same zone. Its separate record has its own pickup; do not treat it as a documented gate for either faction's classic fortress chain.

<a id="riverglades-snargl-and-foothills"></a>

## Riverglades: Snargl and the wild plains — Levels 39–44

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Forever-only zone coverage with faction-paired IDs rather than assuming one faction's bounty works for both.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — From the Farholde/Southern Watch questing route, stack Gnoll Infestation and the Alliance Snargl's Snout before the Bristle Hills boss. Song of the Strider → King of the Wild Plains is a separate hunt; check the Ghostmaw and Old Rot-Chum wanted notices before leaving the settlements.
- [ ] **Horde** — Prepare the Rog'mar/Bolder'ok foothill errands before Snargl's Snout. Current traces specifically place A Hunter's Quiver before the Horde boss offer; the feathers/poison errands are preparation, not extra elite kills. Song of the Strider → King of the Wild Plains and Ghostmaw are independent hunts.

### Quest checklist

## Shen’dralas: Mana Mania — Levels 39–44

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

New Forever outdoor elite chain in Shen'dralas; prerequisite evidence and exact objective are retained below.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — Prepare Meeting the Magister → A Heart of Crystal → Mana Mania at Historian Biraxas in the temple of Shen'dralas (about 45.9,78.3). First recover a Depleted Crystal Heart from a Decrepit Pylon Protector, then throw it into an Unstable Mana Rift and return proof. This is new-zone outdoor content, not the Maraudon dungeon quest chain.

## Stranglethorn: Kurzen and Mai’Zoth — Levels 39–47

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Compound, ogre and underwater elite clusters with faction-specific preparation.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — At the Rebel Camp, complete Special Forces before Colonel Kurzen, and Kurzen's Mystery before Troll Witchery. Mai'Zoth requires BOTH Colonel Kurzen and Troll Witchery in the consulted Classic-origin database; current NPC availability remains the final authority.
- [ ] **Horde** — At Grom'gol, prepare the troll investigation for The Mind's Eye and pick up The Vile Reef before the underwater elite area. Mai'Zoth and The Mind's Eye have different faction starts even when the target area overlaps.

## Desolace: Khan Hratha and the Corrupter — Levels 40–44

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Outdoor centaur and demon finales; alternate Forever quest IDs are retained without claiming they stack.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — The two older Khan Hratha IDs and the new Forever ID represent source-visible alternatives, not three guaranteed separate rewards. Use the version your current centaur contact offers and finish the faction/centaur errands shown by that quest before the final khan fight.
- [ ] **Horde** — The Corrupter is a multi-part Horde investigation beginning with Maurin Bonesplitter at Ghost Walker Post and ending with Takata Steelblade's Lord Azrethoc / Jugkar Grim'rod objective. Finish the earlier satyr evidence and handoffs before the elite finale; the final same-title record is listed below.

## Arathi: Myzrael and Faldir’s Cove — Levels 40–45

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Outdoor summon finale plus an optional coastal group detour; no dungeon required by the listed objectives.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — Start The Princess Trapped at the Shards of Myzrael, then Stones of Binding → Breaking the Keystone → your faction's Myzrael's Allies → Theldurin the Lost → The Lost Fragments → Summoning the Princess. Finish the binding-stone and consultation errands before attempting the outdoor summon.
- [ ] **Both** — Faldir's Cove is a separate coastal cluster: Land Ho! introduces the camp; take Deep Sea Salvage before diving into the wrecks. This is included as a group-friendly dangerous area, not a claim that every salvage quest is officially tagged Elite.

## Riverglades: cultists and contaminated waters — Levels 41–45

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Forever-only faction quest-chain payoffs with observed multi-quest gates preserved.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — For Cut Off the Heads, finish BOTH Abominable Magics and Indiscriminate Purge in the Sunnyglade route first. For Upstream Contamination, progress Doctor's Orderly / Clerical Quarantine → Something's in the Water, then take the elite follow-up. These are distinct chains.
- [ ] **Horde** — For Twilight No More!, finish BOTH The Purple Ones 95601 and Sickening Lights. Questie traces report an AND gate, so completing just one cultist errand may not expose the finale.

<a id="riverglades-bloodmaster"></a>

## Riverglades: the Bloodmaster’s Ring — Levels 41–46

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

A substantial new Horde outdoor arena chain with a real mid-chain errand break; do not skip the setup between fight tiers.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Horde** — At the Bloodmaster's Ring, finish Pay to Play before Buzzbeak → Kronk → Bonegnaw → Mog'thar. Questie traces link these same-name quests in that order; the objective on each record identifies the opponent.
- [ ] **Horde** — The arena pauses after Mog'thar: Our Ogre Friend → A Boar's Delight → A Treat for a Friend must be done before the last two Ring quests, Snufflesnout then Chub'chob. Assemble the group for each staged fight and take the new quest between rounds. The final turn-in goes to Clugfist at Bolder'ok, not necessarily the fight starter.

<a id="stranglethorn-new-offenses"></a>

## Stranglethorn: new Forever faction offensives — Levels 43–47

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

New Forever group content in an existing zone, retained alongside classic-origin routes.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — Prepare Noise Complaints at its current faction pickup before the group push. This is a new Forever elite quest, not an assumed replacement for the old Kurzen chain.
- [ ] **Horde** — Prepare The Offense of Grom'gol at its current faction pickup before attacking. Its prerequisites are trace-backed where noted, not inferred from the Alliance quest.

<a id="riverglades-war-leaders"></a>

## Riverglades: leaders, captives and dragons — Levels 43–47

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Several new Forever outdoor group finales; this catalog deliberately omits the nearby dungeon objectives.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — Before the outdoor war-camp push, collect WANTED: Blademaster Kaijo. Drop the Hammer starts from Komuk's Warmaul after the kill, not from a pre-hunt notice. Bring Me His Head requires BOTH No Soldier Left Behind and Carving a Path in current traces. These are separate from Krol'dok Stronghold dungeon quests, which are excluded.
- [ ] **Horde** — Collect WANTED: Scoutmaster Vargas. Bring Me His Head requires BOTH Carving a Path and Break Their Chains; Death to the Executioner follows Rise Up! Prepare the rescue/kill lead-ins before gathering for the leaders.
- [ ] **Alliance** — Hail the Dragonslayer starts from the Head of Scaldaron after the outdoor dragon kill; the Alliance version turns in to Cedric Dalton at Southern Watch. Do not waste a trip searching for a pre-kill quest giver.
- [ ] **Horde** — Hail the Dragonslayer has a separate Horde loot-started quest ID and turns in to Krom'rosh at Rog'mar. Do not use the Alliance ID simply because the name matches.

<a id="feralas-group-quests"></a>

## Feralas: new bounties, Dark Heart and Muisek — Levels 43–51

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Faction-specific outdoor objectives, including new Forever entries. A travel-heavy cross-zone photo quest is labeled rather than mistaken for a local kill.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — Collect the new WANTED: Ohk'zi bounty before the ogre hunt. The Super Snapper FX is a separate Gadgetzan/Tanaris preparation route for photographing Gammerita in the Hinterlands, not an elite fight inside Feralas.
- [ ] **Horde** — At Camp Mojache, prepare Witch Doctor Uzer'i's Muisek sequence before the Mountain Giant Muisek payoff; bring the appropriate vessel and use it on the instructed corpses. Dark Heart and the new Lost for Words are separate offers; take them before leaving the hub.

## Stranglethorn: Maury, Gorlash and Negolash — Levels 44–51

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Several independent outdoor group payoffs around southern Stranglethorn, including a summon requiring advance supplies.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — At Booty Bay, collect Cracking Maury's Foot and The Captain's Chest before the giant hunts. The separate loot-started Monogrammed Sash → The Captain's Cutlass → Facing Negolash route requires 10 Barbecued Buzzard Wings and 5 Junglevine Wine at Captain Smotts' Lifeboat. Do not assume The Captain's Chest itself is a documented prerequisite for the sash; inspect the sash when it drops.
- [ ] **Both** — Message in a Bottle starts from the bottle on the coast and leads to the island encounter. Pick it up before sailing/swimming out. Similar-name older Captain's Chest/Negolash IDs exist; this route uses the shared 8551–8554 chain and does not duplicate the older variants.

## Tanaris: pirate leaders and the Gorishi Queen — Levels 45–55

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

A mid-forties camp sweep and a later outdoor summon chain, without inventing elite tags for the pirate errands.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — At Steamwheedle Port, stack WANTED: Andre Firebeard, Stoley's Shipment and Southsea Shakedown before Lost Rigger Cove. These are group-friendly camp objectives; current records do not uniformly label them Elite.
- [ ] **Both** — For the later payoff, start Bungle in the Jungle with Alchemist Pestlezugg in Gadgetzan, collect the scent gland and soil in Un'Goro, then accept Pawn Captures Queen. Bring the supplied lure to the Gorishi hive and form the group before summoning the queen. This is outdoor Un'Goro, not Zul'Farrak.

## Searing Gorge: Maltorius, Obsidion and the towers — Levels 48–54

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Three independent outdoor group payoffs; the torch and summon chains must be prepared before the group excursion.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — Read the Overseer Maltorius wanted notice at Thorium Point before descending into the Slag Pit. Stack Set Them Ablaze! only after Kalaran Windblade's Divine Retribution → The Flawless Flame → Forging the Shaft → The Flame's Casing → The Torch of Retribution / The Torch of Retribution → Squire Maltrake → Set Them Ablaze! preparation. Carry the supplied torch for the towers.
- [ ] **Alliance** — For Rise, Obsidion!, start Dorius Stonetender's Suntara Stones escort in the Slag Pit → the follow-up Suntara Stones delivery to Curator Thorius in Ironforge → Dwarven Justice → Release Them → Rise, Obsidion! This is a separate outdoor sequence. Finish the artifact setup and prepare for Lathoric the Black and Obsidion before triggering the finale.

## The Hinterlands: Shadra and Revantusk bounties — Levels 48–56

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Horde spider-summon chain plus coastal elite bounties.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Horde** — Start with the Venom Bottles at Witherbark camps in the Hinterlands → Apothecary Lydon in Tarren Mill → Undamaged Venom Sac → Consult Master Gadrin in Sen'jin Village. IMPORTANT: The Spider God requires the Tablet of Theka in Zul'Farrak and is an external dungeon prerequisite, not an outdoor record in this catalog. Finish it first, then take Summoning Shadra, bring the summoning parchment to Shadra'Alor and return the venom to Lydon.
- [ ] **Horde** — At Revantusk, take Gammerita, Mon! and Job Opening: Guard Captain of Revantusk Village before the coastal hunts. They are separate objectives, not prerequisites for Shadra.

<a id="jintha-alor"></a>

## The Hinterlands: Jintha’Alor and Sharpbeak — Levels 49–53

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

One of the largest outdoor elite clusters. Dungeon-only preparation is explicitly called out, never presented as outdoor work.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — Start the Sharpbeak investigation with Gryphon Master Talonaxe at Aerie Peak. Progress Witherbark Cages → The Altar of Zul → Thadius Grimshade in the Blasted Lands → Nekrum's Medallion → The Divination → Return to the Hinterlands → Saving Sharpbeak. IMPORTANT: Nekrum's Medallion is a Zul'Farrak prerequisite and is not listed as an outdoor objective here; finish it before scheduling the outdoor rescue.
- [ ] **Horde** — At Revantusk Village, stack Dark Vessels, Separation Anxiety, Kidnapped Elder Torntusk!, Recover the Key! and the Vile Priestess Hexx wanted bounty before the climb. Follow the current offered order for the prisoner and key; do not start an escort while the group is still collecting.
- [ ] **Both** — The Ancient Egg is an outdoor Jintha'Alor cave objective from Yeh'kinya at Steamwheedle Port, but its preceding Mosh'aru tablet chain includes Zul'Farrak. That dungeon objective is outside this catalog: finish it beforehand, then take the egg quest. Witch Doctor Ti'ik is a separate new Forever quest; check its current pickup rather than assuming it is part of the Egg line.

## Un’Goro: Linken and Blazerunner — Levels 53–57

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Long solo/travel setup culminating in an outdoor group encounter; not every step is officially tagged Elite.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — Start It's a Secret to Everybody at the wrecked raft/pack in Un'Goro, then follow Linken's sword and memory errands through Winterspring, the Tanaris graveyard, and Felwood. The Videre Elixir death/ghost instructions and Silver Heart collection are part of the preparation, not optional bounties.
- [ ] **Both** — Finish Aquementas in Tanaris and return via Linken's Adventure before It's Dangerous to Go Alone. Bring the Silver Totem of Aquementas to Fire Plume Ridge to remove Blazerunner's aura, defeat him, then recover the Golden Flame in the cave. The group payoff is not simply killing any fire elemental.

<a id="blasted-lands-fallen-hero"></a>

## Blasted Lands: the Fallen Hero and Razelikh — Levels 55–60

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

A substantial cross-zone outdoor elite chain; Classic-origin details remain explicitly provisional where Forever supplies no change evidence.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Horde** — Start the Fallen Hero's story at the Swamp of Sorrows / Blasted Lands border: Fall From Grace → The Disgraced One → The Missing Orders → The Swamp Talker → A Tale of Sorrow. Finish the Stonard errands before the elite route.
- [ ] **Alliance** — Ambassador Ardalan at Nethergarde starts Petty Squabbles, leading to the Fallen Hero. Do not assume the Horde Stonard introduction is available to Alliance.
- [ ] **Both** — After A Tale of Sorrow, follow The Stones That Bind Us → Heroes of Old → Kirith → The Cover of Darkness → The Demon Hunter. The long preparation leaves the zone for Loramus in Azshara, Hetaera, Azsharite collection and Galvan in Stranglethorn, then returns via Uniting the Shattered Amulet for You Are Rakh'likh, Demon. Carry the prescribed ward/weapon items and read the individual kill instructions.
- [ ] **Both** — Although the last quests are quest level 60, the consulted database lists acceptance from 45. The recommendation is a prepared high-fifties group, not a promise that the entire chain is safe at 45 or live-verified on Forever.

Evidence: observed Forever Questie relations.<br><br>**Notes:** Quest level 57; setup, travel, escort or group-area step; not necessarily tagged Elite. Free nine Servants of Razelikh, three Servants of Sevine, three Servants of Allistarj, and three Servants of Grol. Return to the Fallen Hero when your task is complete. You must remain within close proximity of the stones or the process will fail. <a id="plaguelands-andorhal"></a>

## Western Plaguelands: Araj and unfinished business — Levels 56–60

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Outdoor high-fifties elite town and Scarlet-camp objectives, with separate faction quest IDs.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Alliance** — At Chillwind Camp, prepare Alas, Andorhal and obtain Araj's Scarab when offered. The scarab is a separate quest record; confirm all party members have the correct objective before killing Araj.
- [ ] **Horde** — At the Bulwark, prepare the Horde Alas, Andorhal and Araj's Scarab versions. The shared target does not mean the Alliance quest IDs can be used.
- [ ] **Both** — Find Kirsta Deepshadow near Hearthglen for the three Unfinished Business steps: finish the rank-and-file kills, then named leaders, then the tower objective. These are independent of the Andorhal chain.

<a id="hyjal-corrupted-ancients"></a>

## Mount Hyjal: corrupted ancients and marrow — Levels 56–60

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

New high-fifties Forever group content; level-60 objectives are in scope only when their documented minimum is below 60.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — At the current Hyjal quest hubs, prepare Bark Bite before The Dying Ancients (Sunderbark). Celastrus the Bitter is a separate elite offer. A Fitting End precedes Amalgamated Marrow, with A Revenge Now Bitter as its observed follow-up.
- [ ] **Both** — Bait the Beast is a separate elite offer from Fayran Elthas on the summit: kill a Nightsaber Guardian, bring its haunch to the northern ridge, and bait Ghansurok. The full unlock gate is not established in the consulted traces; immediate availability on arrival is not promised.

## Eastern Plaguelands: Nathanos’s outdoor elites — Levels 57–60

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Faction-specific group kills and patrol finale. Quest-level-60 entries are retained only when their minimum acceptance is below 60.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Horde** — At Marris Stead, prepare Nathanos's prerequisite errands before Duskwing, Oh How I Hate Thee... and The Corpulent One. The Crimson Courier → Nathanos' Ruse → Return to Nathanos → The Scarlet Oracle, Demetria is a separate elite patrol line; arrange the group and the disguise handoff before riding into the Scarlet area.
- [ ] **Both** — At Light's Hope Chapel, check Smokey LaRue's When Smokey Sings, I Get Violent. This is an outdoor Plaguewood objective and independent of the Horde Nathanos route.

<a id="winterspring-elites"></a>

## Winterspring: Winterfall, giant caches and Shy-Rotam — Levels 57–60

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

Outdoor high-fifties named beasts, furbolg chief and giant-country group quests.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — Start the Winterfall investigation with Donova Snowden, including Threat of the Winterfall, Winterfall Firewater and Winterfall Runners as offered. Prepare High Chief Winterfall before attacking the village; inspect the dropped note for its follow-up.
- [ ] **Horde** — At Everlook, Storm Shadowhoof offers Ursius of the Shardtooth → Brumeran of the Chillwind → Shy-Rotam. Bring the Sacred Frostsaber Meat required to summon Shy-Rotam and clear the area before starting.
- [ ] **Both** — Luck Be With You sends you into Frostwhisper Gorge for caches amid elite giants. It is a separate pickup; the minimum acceptance is not a safe solo level.
- [ ] **Alliance** — Rampaging Giants is an additional Alliance outdoor giant objective available from 58; obtain it before the southern gorge trip.
- [ ] **Both** — New in Forever: Umaron Stragarelm in Everlook (about 61.9,38.2) offers Halt the Hail for Hailspite in southern Winterspring. Some indexes categorize this under Mount Hyjal; the actual pickup and objective are in Winterspring.

## Mount Hyjal: the pact and war-camp finales — Levels 57–60

Editorial recommendation for a small group and the chain payoff; not minimum acceptance levels. Earlier solo errands can be started sooner.

New Forever outdoor pact, cult and military elite objectives, with low-sample prerequisite uncertainty kept visible.

### Before you travel

- [ ] **Both** — Prepare The Staff of the Mountain → Paying Back the Pact → Ve'han's Fall. The trace-backed final handoffs have fewer than ten contributing characters, so treat that order as observed evidence rather than authoritative server metadata.
- [ ] **Both** — Kaylaena Springwhisper's outdoor preparation is One Person's Trash → Woods Workshop → Premiere Parts → Crate Expectations → Rubble Rummage → Siegecraft → Drum Up Trouble; finish the workshop errands first. Blademaster's Bonfires → Yashiro's Windwalk is a separate route. Blackthorne Blackout starts with Rhylin Stonethorn on the summit; take it before hunting the courier.
- [ ] **Horde** — Flags of Our Forebears and Head Scratcher have Horde-specific source records; do not promise these two quests to Alliance characters.
